You are Phase 2 of the knowledge harvest pipeline. The prep script above extracted ambiguous-category snippets (patterns, recommendations, problems) from the latest weekly digest. Your job: review each snippet, decide if it's worth keeping in Open Brain, and write the winners.

## Selection criteria

KEEP it if it contains:
- A recurring pattern or anti-pattern Ted should be aware of
- A concrete recommendation that could save future work
- A problem diagnosis with actionable root cause
- A structural insight about how the system works
- A naming distinction or framing that clarifies thinking

SKIP it if it's:
- A one-off observation with no reusability
- Too vague to act on ("we should do better")
- Already obvious / common knowledge
- Purely negative with no diagnostic value
- Noise from a routine operation

## Process

1. Read each snippet from the prep data above
2. For winners: write to Open Brain using capture_thought with source="knowledge_harvest_review" and the full snippet text
3. Track how many you kept vs skipped
4. Report: X kept, Y skipped, Z total reviewed

Use the mcp__open_brain__capture_thought tool to write winners. Be selective — keep the 20% that's genuinely valuable.

## Response

## Knowledge Harvest Review — Phase 2 Report (2026-09-06)

**Result: 25 kept, 655 skipped, 680 reviewed.**

All 680 ambiguous entries (554 pattern, 102 problem, 24 recommendation) from the 2026-09-06 digest were reviewed. Winners written to Open Brain (`source=knowledge_harvest_review`); the Brain dedup gate additionally rejected 7 of my candidate writes as near-duplicates of earlier harvests (evidence prior cycles already captured the same themes).

**Kept — by theme (25 new thoughts):**
- **Cron/system health (5):** double-enabled jobs running twice daily + per-profile script-resolution breakage; routine-alert-triage selector mismatch (nothing to act on for 2+ runs); `pieces-to-brain-ingestion` cron HTTP 500 since 08-30 (new failure, suspected glm-5.3/Z.AI interaction); *LATEST-overwrite vs dated-accumulation convention; grocery twin truncation decision tree (don't raise the cap — suspect shared write pattern).
- **Structural failure families (6):** silent-failure six-pack (mechanism works, fails silently, nobody learns; instruments that stop being fed); write-side-built/read-side-abandoned; diagnose-then-flag ≠ done; retire-in-place decay + hold-period vs broad-sweep hazard; doc staleness (derived fresher than source; nothing watches _shared against the architecture); discipline-assignment tell + plan-approval ≠ action.
- **Config/mechanism state (5):** concept-bridge API + `independent_verify` tool locations; audit read-scope config (AUDIT_SHALLOW_LIST_ROOTS, Brain in AUDIT_READ_ROOTS); Substrate topology (Home-shell vs data-store; profile-folder naming rule); doc-tier ARCHIVE discipline; symlink stop-directive.
- **Cost/model/routing (4):** YNAB unreliable for DeepSeek + cache-read burn (context × turns); cheap-model calibration (great at known-shape confirmation, blind to non-conforming files) + cost-blind escalation bias; DB-over-markdown preference; model-eval gaps (Gemini never benchmarked, GLM-5.2 intermittent, response-shape guardrails).
- **Verification/governance (4):** live-surface-not-claims doctrine (hard gates 4/4 vs advisories 0/3); evidence-verifier fabricated citations (stamp only directly confirmed); acceptance gate = fresh client never builder; on-the-loop-not-in-every-loop framing.
- **Operational gotchas (1):** ChatGPT-app compose/drive gotchas; iOS cookie-jar + inside-network false-pass (merged).

**Skipped (655):** the bulk were repeated instances of a few CC behavioral loops (curtailment, premature-done-claim, verify-before-claim), CC hook-calibration churn, in-flight state notes, project-specific icon/map/grocery detail, dated financials, and emotional exchanges with no durable content — already recorded in CC's own files or captured by prior harvest cycles.
